I don't see anyone or anything that isn't there. And I don't feel anything touching me, either. My thoughts usually make perfect sense to me. I don't really have trouble keeping things straight in my head... I know what I'm trying to think about or what I need to do. It's really just those voices that are the problem, but my own mind feels organized enough.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had beliefs or ideas that others might not share, such as thinking that you have special powers, that you're being watched, or that certain events have a special meaning just for you?\n\nPatient: Well... yes, I suppose. I mean, I got it in my head that the other home-care agency in town, and some people from the library, were watching me. I was sure they were looking at my paintings and my client papers, trying to steal this... this chance that was meant for me. People told me it didn't make any sense, but... I still knew they were doing it. It just felt so real to me.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about your belief that people from the home-care agency and the library were watching you and trying to steal something meant for you? What exactly made you feel that way, and how certain were you that it was happening?\n\nPatient: Like I said a minute ago, I was just... I was completely sure of it. I knew they had their eyes on my paintings and were looking at my client papers. They wanted to steal that chance that was supposed to be mine. \n\nFolks kept telling me I was wrong, that it didn't make a lick of sense for the other agency or the library people to care about my things or spy on me. But... I didn't care what they said. I just knew they were doing it. I was a hundred percent certain, even if no one else believed me.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me how you came to be so certain that these people were watching you and trying to steal your chance? Did something specific happen, or did the feeling come on gradually over time?\n\nPatient: I don't know if there was one specific thing that started it, honestly. It just... I just got it in my head that the other home-care agency and some people from the library were watching my paintings and my client papers. Even when folks told me it made no sense and tried to talk me out of it, I still knew they were doing it. I just knew.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me if you’ve ever questioned your belief that these people were watching you and trying to steal your chance, even for a moment, or has it always felt completely true and unshakable to you?\n\nPatient: No, I never really questioned it. I still knew they were doing it. It just felt so real to me.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about your belief that people from the home-care agency and the library were watching you and trying to steal something meant for you? What exactly made you feel that way, and how certain were you that it was happening?\n\nPatient: Like I said a minute ago, I was just... I was completely sure of it. I knew they had their eyes on my paintings and were looking at my client papers. They wanted to steal that chance that was supposed to be mine. \n\nFolks kept telling me I was wrong, that it didn't make a lick of sense for the other agency or the library people to care about my things or spy on me.
